Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the intervals object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 729. The moduli space has dimension at most 282 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on intervals structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 729. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 282. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the intervals space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 599 and the convergence rate is O(526/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 599 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the intervals structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 526. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
